### 2026-09-11

_Lesson is almost entirely one verb pair (bi5rab / bi5arreb) drilled; few free sentences, so vocab rows are few. Holes where Medi's Arabic is lost as [speaking Arabic]: 18:14-18:46 (AC sentence; Amal 'كما ما دا' = engine for كمان مرة, then 'خرب', 'ممتاز'), 20:49/21:08 (sentence Amal recast as بتستعمل / بتستعمله), 22:42, 30:38 (Amal then supplied 'ما أجا' - something in his clause was wrong, cannot quote it), 31:32-31:57 (the command exchange: his lines missing, Amal 'ما تخربيه. خلاص، ممتاز'), 33:43, 34:01, 35:36, 36:21, 54:31-55:06 (photos: he wavered خربوا / خِرِب, Amal's Arabic lost), 53:11, 53:50.
